10900- *
10901- * ALAC and AC-4 go to FFmpeg on every device, because MediaCodec is never
10902- * offered them ([withoutUnreliablePlatformDecoders]); the platform AC-4 decoders proved
10903- * unreliable, much as the ALAC ones below did. Samsung ships no ALAC codec, which is where FFmpeg
10904- * was proved; Qualcomm vendor images ship `c2.qti.alac.sw.decoder`, which
10905- * released itself on its first input buffer (realme RMX3842, Android 16) and
10906- * misreports position on the tracks it does play. A codec that fails mid-
10907- * playback is never retried on the next renderer — the track just falls to
10908- * YouTube — so the only fix is not to start there. Nothing is given up: the
10909- * vendor codec is software as well, and lossless decoders agree to the bit.
10910- *
10911- * Shared by the real players and the upgrade audition, which must be able to
10912- * decode whatever it is auditioning or it can never prove it — and with the
10913- * same decoder, or the proof is of something else.
10914- */
